================ RECONCILIATION FLAGS (for the code2wav converter) ================
  has code_embedding : True
  has quantizer/rvq  : False   <- TTS uses decoder.quantizer.rvq_first/rvq_rest; if False, Omni decodes codes differently
  has pre_transformer: True
  has upsample       : True
  has decoder blocks : True

Next: map each code2wav.* name -> the A_GEN_WAV_* tensor it feeds in qwen3tts-gen.cpp::code2wav (compare to qwen3tts.py::_generate_code2wav_tensors).
